It happens on every resume, since the launcher builds a new timestamped run_dir each time. The proposed fix seeds `artifacts.history` and `metrics.csv` on resume from the source run's `metrics.csv` rows with step <= saved_step (located through `checkpoint_path.parent.parent`), or at least records `source_run_dir` in `summary.json` beside `best_step`. A resumed run directory whose `metrics.csv` contains the pre-resume rows would falsify the finding. Reported by gap-checkpoint-resume-exactness; new.

### 124. predictive_covariance_checkpoint headroom: moment-matched update saves 1.94 GiB per layer at B=32 (info)

This is reported as headroom, not as a defect, at `vfe3/model/relational_operators.py:1331-1334`. The investigator ran a `saved_tensors_hooks` probe by region (`probe_saved.py`, CPU, live shape at B=1, scaled x32; executed). The covariance update saved 1.94 GiB per layer, out of 3.29 GiB per layer in total. With `predictive_covariance_checkpoint` on, the covariance update saves nothing and the layer total is 1.35 GiB, about 7.8 GiB less over 4 layers. The live run's `peak_mem_mb` is 17.9-20.3 GB of 32 GB, so memory does not bind at B=32. By the investigator's count, the toggle recomputes about 114 GFLOP per layer of IEEE-fp32 work in backward. The toggle is False on the launcher, and no fix is needed at B=32. It should be turned on only if a larger batch or width is wanted, and its docstring says values are bit-identical. The measurement would be falsified if `torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated` on the live dict differed by much less than 7 GiB between the two settings. Reported by performance; new as a measurement, and the toggle's docstring already states "about half the activation memory of a large-K step".

### 125. Evaluation and diagnostic forwards run outside deferred_host_checks and pay one host sync per certificate (info)

The locations are `vfe3/train.py:3726-3729`, `vfe3/train.py:1433`, `vfe3/train.py:1442` and `vfe3/contracts.py:42-51`. `train_step` wraps its forward in `deferred_host_checks()` (`train.py:833, 868`). `_eval_forward_with_precision_retry` does not, so `host_check` runs `verdict(flags.tolist())` immediately. The investigator counted `host_check` calls on `live_config` tiny with 2 layers (executed). The evaluate forward made 5 immediate checks and 0 queued, while `train_step` made 0 immediate and 3 queued. The sliding-window validation runs about 4x more forwards than disjoint windows, so the stalls grow with it. The GPU cost was not measured. This affects every `evaluate()` call on the active config: validation at stride 32, test, and the fixed train sample. The proposed fix runs `call()` inside `deferred_host_checks()` in `_no_update_call_with_precision_retry`. The scope closes before any CE is read on the host, and a raised verdict still reaches the precision ladder. A CUDA profile showing no stream stalls from `host_check` during evaluate would falsify the finding. Reported by training; new (`docs/2026-10-12.md` item 22 reduced training-step syncs only).

### 126. GaugeManifoldAdamW's cumulative phi_rejected_rows counter is neither checkpointed nor read (info)

At `vfe3/gauge_optim.py:1505-1516` and `vfe3/gauge_optim.py:1745-1750`, `self._phi_rejected_rows` adds a device tensor on every committed pullback step through `self._phi_rejected_rows = (rejected if ... else self._phi_rejected_rows + rejected)`, and the count is exposed as the `phi_rejected_rows` property. A repository-wide grep finds readers only in tests (`test_audit_20261008_geometry.py:216`, `test_phi_fisher.py:475`, `test_remediation_20260923_C.py:165`). The counter is missing from `RUN_TOTAL_COUNTER_KEYS` (`train.py:2107-2111`) and from the optimizer state_dict, so it restarts at 0 on resume. What runs actually record is the per-step `phi_group_rejected_rows` metrics column (`train.py:5216`). The evidence comes from reading the code. It applies only to the GaugeManifoldAdamW route, `m_phi_update_mode='pullback_group'` or `'fisher_group'`, and not to the active adamw route. The proposed fix either adds the count to the checkpointed run totals and to `summary.json` `numerics_counters`, or removes the cumulative accumulation and keeps the per-step column. A production consumer of `phi_rejected_rows` (summary, metrics or checkpoint) found by grep would falsify the finding. Reported by gap-checkpoint-resume-exactness; new.

### 127. No whole-model test pins the fully_gauge_covariant switch or passive local-gauge invariance across groups (info)

The relevant files are `tests/test_fully_gauge_covariant_20261014.py:1-9`, `tests/test_decode_coordinates_20260928.py:310-341` and `vfe3/viz/extract.py:705-920`. The switch test is config-level only ("Config-level only (no model is built)"). `projected_gauge_equivariance` covers the relational operators per layer, and `test_functional_stack_is_covariant_under_block_frame_changes` covers one block_glk layer. The investigator ran a float64 probe through `VFEModel.forward` on the tiny live route, with the switch applied and every parameter perturbed off init (executed). In the probe, `prior_bank.encode` was patched to (g_i mu_i, g_i Sigma_i g_i^T, Log(g_i U_i)), with a per-position g_i = exp(s a.G) in the live group at s = 0.1 and 0.3. The maximum relative logit change was at most 7e-11 and the loss change at most 3e-14 for block_glk per_head/shared, block_so, block_co, block_sl, block_borel, block_unipotent, block_glmc, so_n shared/per_head (l1+l2) and su_n shared/per_head (pq1_0+pq1_1). The same held for pos_phi='learned', prototype_agents, gaussian_template, gauge_gate/canonical_frame (local), linear decode, frame RoPE (switched to comparison), lowrank/pure_gauge/latent edge links, minimal_agent (mean_field and exact), gaussian_block, and CG coupling on so_n/su_n. A beliefs-only negative control moved logits by 4e-3 to 5.7, and the identity-patch baseline was 1e-14 to 5e-11. One artifact came from the probe itself: eig-based logm failed for unipotent frames and was replaced by `vfe3.geometry.matrix_log.logm_principal`. So the property holds when executed; the gap is test coverage of the gauge facet the switch certifies. The proposed fix adds a parametrized float64 test that patches `prior_bank.encode` with a per-position group draw (Log via `logm_principal`) and asserts logit and loss invariance, plus a beliefs-only negative control, over the registered groups and tyings. A route admitted by `gauge_covariance_failures()==[]` whose logits move by more than 1e-8 relative under the encode-patch probe would falsify the invariance claim. Reported by gauge-groups; new.

### 128. No resume-equivalence test covers the live optimizer routing (fisher tables, group clip, moment-matched route, frame bound, untied decode) (info)

The relevant files are `tests/test_checkpoint_resume.py:52-58`, `tests/test_checkpoint_resume.py:661-688`, `tests/test_checkpoint_resume.py:1321-1361`, `tests/profiles.py:143-163` and `tests/test_table_fisher_20261010.py:141`. Every resume-equivalence test builds `_cfg(...)` on `test_config`. That is the legacy baseline, with `'m_table_update_mode': 'adamw'`, `'grad_clip_scope': 'role'`, `'attention_mode': 'projected_gradient'` and `'predictive_covariance': 'passthrough'` (`profiles.py:71-163`). The table-Fisher test uses checkpoint_interval=0. The investigator's probe filled the gap and found no defect (executed). The probe used `live_config`: TableFisherAdamW, grad_clip_scope='group', projected_predictive with moment_matched covariance, phi_frame_condition_max=1e4, untied decode, per-epoch window origin and a shuffled generator. Under it, the step_8 checkpoints of the continuous run and of resumes at step 4 (epoch boundary) and step 6 (mid-epoch) matched bitwise in every leg (0 differing legs). The same held with eval and logging every 2 steps, with `m_phi_update_mode='fisher_group'` (TableFisherGaugeManifoldAdamW), and with `amp_dtype='fp16'` (GradScaler). Nothing pins this route against regression. The proposed fix adds a parametrized test on `tests.profiles.live_config` (checkpoint_interval=2, 8 steps, shuffled TokenWindows loader, resume at an epoch boundary and mid-epoch, phi mode adamw/fisher_group) that compares the final checkpoints leg by leg, using `probe_resume.py` as the template. An existing test that resumes a `live_config` or `m_table_update_mode='fisher'` run and compares it with an uninterrupted run would falsify the finding. Reported by gap-checkpoint-resume-exactness; new.

## Checked and cleared

The investigators examined the items below and found them correct; none of them went through adjudication.

### recent-diff

The `_SpectralCeiling` Daleckii-Krein backward at `vfe3/model/covariance_ceiling.py:57-78` passes `torch.autograd.gradcheck` in float64 for random W straddling the ceiling and for near-degenerate eigenvalue pairs with 1e-9 gaps, and the dense ceiling output satisfies min eig(cSigma_p - out) = -4.5e-15. At `vfe3/model/covariance_ceiling.py:128` the anchor is detached, so the fix for finding 1 of the 2026-10-15 audit is present, and the diagonal and head-block paths keep the family layout. The failing-sequence fp64 escalation at `vfe3/model/relational_operators.py:1265-1322` is correct in its per-sequence versus shared frame split, per-run receive lists, floor slicing and `index_copy` gradients; an fp64 failure raises, and the tests pass. The `relative_floor_term` at `vfe3/model/relational_operators.py:942-978` equals, in exact arithmetic, the pre-5f5d484 ridge f tr(C)/K I inside C_att, with the blockwise, dense and minimal-agent forms compared against `git show 5f5d484^`. In `vfe3/config.py:2157-2172`, `inactive_fields` marks floor_mode, the ceiling and escalation inert off moment_matched and off the kernel readout. Applied to the active `train_vfe3.py` config, `fully_gauge_covariant_overrides` (`vfe3/gauge_covariant.py:33-95`) gives {decode_coordinates, sigma_max, family->gaussian_full, decode_mode->full_chunked, block_coordinates->reference}; the resolved config constructs and `gauge_covariance_failures` is empty, whereas the unmodified config fails 4 flags. In `train_vfe3.py:552-560` and in `ablation.py` `_require_gauge_covariant`/`_resolved_sweep_baseline` the overrides are applied before the seed and the arms, and covariance is re-checked per cell in `validate_sweeps` and `run_single`. The `RUN_TOTAL_COUNTER_KEYS` at `vfe3/train.py:2107-2125` include `covariance_ceiling_elements` and `covariance_escalation_elements`, are reset at the start of `train()` and resume from the checkpoint base. `_parameter_clip_scales` at `vfe3/train.py:704-718` matches the `clip_grads_with_norm_` coefficients, and the spike attribution recovers the pre-clip role norm on AdamW tables (the test passes). The grad-accumulation second-order objective at `vfe3/train.py:886-901` uses the scored-token denominator, consistent with the CE. On the Triton decode path in `vfe3/model/prior_bank.py`, ignore_index targets never index memory in `kl_ce_fwd`/`bwd` (the hit is comparison-only) and are masked by `valid` in the mean. `_student_t_quantile_975` in `multiseed_analysis.py` matches tabulated t_{0.975} for nu = 1, 2, 3, 4, 9, 30 to 4 decimals, with the A&S 26.7.3-4 series checked. The per-epoch origin bound b = min(s, T-L) and the window count at `vfe3/data/datasets.py:804-818` match the docstring formula. `_lr_width_transfer_arms` at `ablation.py:1262-1293` is strict only when enabled, and inert rates are refused by the all-arms-inert check in `validate_sweeps`. The focused test files `test_config_isolation`, `test_live_route_profile`, `test_ablation_lr_width_transfer_20261015`, `test_covariance_ceiling_20261013`, `test_predictive_covariance_floor_mode_20261016`, `test_divergence_spike_attribution_20261015` and `test_predictive_covariance_fp64_escalation_20261015` pass on CPU. The WIP fontTools logger change at `vfe3/viz/figure_worker.py:544` has `logging` imported, and the WIP log-message suffixes in `vfe3/train.py` do not break the spike-log test.

### gauge-groups

Every registered group's basis spans the claimed algebra (`vfe3/geometry/generators.py:13-237`, `vfe3/geometry/groups.py:540-1075`): in executed checks at d = 3/4 and H = 2, the flattened rank equals n_gen equals the expected dimension for block_glk (per_head 18, shared 9), block_so 6, block_co 8, block_sl 16, block_borel 12, block_unipotent 6 and block_glmc 16. The exponential lands in the group (`vfe3/geometry/transport.py:771-814`, executed): det is 1 per block for block_so, block_sl, block_unipotent, so_n and su_n, the orthogonality residual is at most 9e-15 for skew groups, and off-block entries are exactly 0.0 for every group. The su(3) irreps pq1_0, pq1_1, pq2_0, pq2_1, pq2_2 and pq3_0 have bracket-homomorphism residual at most 6.2e-15 with diagonal, uniform Gram (executed), and the real-type construction J = P o conj and the complex-type realification are correct (`vfe3/geometry/irreps.py:625-704`). `su_casimir` matches (N^2-1)/(2N), N and 10/3 for SU(3) (2,0) by hand, and the Hodge star squares to (-1)^m on Lambda^m R^{2m} (`vfe3/geometry/irreps.py:482-553`). The CG coupling intertwines (`vfe3/model/cg_coupling.py:230-313`, `vfe3/geometry/cg.py:49-129`, executed): f(rho(g) mu) = rho(g) f(mu) to at most 3.5e-15, and the delta_full covariance pushes forward as rho(g) Sigma rho(g)^T to at most 4.3e-15 for so_n SO(3) [l1 x2, l2], SO(4) [l1, wp2, wm2] and su_n [pq1_1 x2], [pq1_0, pq1_1], [pq1_0, pq1_1, pq1_0] with random path weights. `use_cg_coupling` requires gauge_tying='shared' and delta_full requires gaussian_full, since CG commutes only with a tied gauge (`vfe3/config.py:1245-1262`). Passive local-gauge invariance of logits and loss holds end to end under the fully_gauge_covariant overrides for every group and tying (executed; see the test finding) at `vfe3/gauge_covariant.py:33-95`. The frame actions xi = U^-1 mu and C = U^-1 Sigma U^-T and their block and compact layouts are the correct congruences (read, and covered by the end-to-end probe) at `vfe3/model/frame_actions.py:65-234`. RoPE right insertion V_i = U_i R_i^T keeps Omega_ij = V_i V_j^-1 a flat coboundary covariant under U_i -> g_i U_i, RoPE rotates adjacent pairs (2k, 2k+1) consistently with block_glmc's complex structure, and `contains_pair_rotations` is False exactly for groups lacking rotations (`vfe3/geometry/transport.py:250-330`, `vfe3/geometry/rope.py:79-103`, `vfe3/geometry/groups.py:1020-1075`). Omega_ii = I is re-imposed exactly inside an autocast-disabled island on the dense flat route (`vfe3/geometry/transport.py:946-952`). `project_phi_to_slk` uses the joint (V V^T)^+ solve, which is correct for tied bases, and block_glmc trace removal fixes |det_C| = 1 via Re tr (`vfe3/geometry/lie_ops.py:761-793`). The group-theory claims in docstrings at `vfe3/geometry/groups.py:928-1075` hold: block_sl exp is not onto (diag(-2, -1/2), Culver), block_borel and block_unipotent exp are diffeomorphisms (real ad-spectrum), Iwasawa gives GL+ = SO B+, B+ acts simply transitively on SPD, and CO(d) exp is onto. The holonomy and cocycle diagnostics are labeled as roundoff monitors of the coboundary rather than flatness certificates, and the pure_gauge edge-link holonomy formulas (g_i F^3 g_i^-1 and g_k F g_k^-1) are correct for their loop conventions (`vfe3/metrics.py:1266-1290`, `vfe3/model/edge_links.py:10-22`, `vfe3/config.py:569-576`). Head mixers (kron(A, I_d)) are removed, so there is no untied-gauge commutation hazard (`vfe3/config.py:2597-2721`). The live adamw route does not materialize the dense elementary basis (an executed spy saw 0 dense reads after a `train_step`), and block-local groups keep the lazy local basis under adamw (`vfe3/geometry/groups.py:65-102`). pullback_group train steps run for shared block_glk, block_glmc, block_co, so_n SO(3) [l2 x2], SO(4) [wp2, wm2] and SO(6) [l1, w3] (executed) at `vfe3/gauge_optim.py:375-520`. Edge links and gaussian_block refuse unequal heads, so the K // n_heads split in edge_links is never reached with an unequal so_n tower (`vfe3/config.py:1298-1304`, `vfe3/config.py:1601-1602`).

### transport-geometry

`retract_spd_full` at `vfe3/geometry/retraction.py:655-735` is the affine-invariant exponential map S^{1/2} expm(S^{-1/2} V S^{-1/2}) S^{1/2}: probe p1 in float64 gave 8.3e-16 relative error against the exact AIRM Exp, the log_S round trip was 1.1e-14 relative to ||V||, congruence equivariance R_{GSG^T}(GVG^T) = G R_S(V) G^T held to 9.5e-16, and the AIRM distance was congruence-invariant to 8.9e-16 on a distance of 2.01 while the Frobenius distance changed from 6.1 to 65.5. The trust region at `vfe3/geometry/retraction.py:707-709` clamps the whitened (AIRM) tangent norm, measured at 0.9999999999998 at trust 1, and `retract_spd_diagonal` matches `retract_spd_full` on diagonal input to 4e-16 with and without a binding trust region. `_loewner_adjoint` at `vfe3/geometry/retraction.py:186-267` is the exact Daleckii-Krein divided difference with an f'(midpoint) repeated limit; gradcheck passed for `retract_spd_full` on a separated spectrum and at Sigma = I, and the forward values are unaffected by any damping. In `natural_gradient` at `vfe3/geometry/retraction.py:774-815` the Fisher metric is half the AIRM, so nat_sigma = 2 Sigma g Sigma (2 sigma^2 g on the diagonal), symmetrized. The custom fixed-schedule exp and its block-triangular adjoint at `vfe3/geometry/transport.py:1137-1259` passed float64 gradcheck and gradgradcheck at d = 4/6/8 with ||A||_F up to 18.9, and in fp32 at d = 32 the relative error against float64 was 2.2e-6 forward and 1.3e-6 for the gradient at ||A||_F = 50, the same as `torch.linalg.matrix_exp`. `stable_matrix_exp_pair` at `vfe3/geometry/transport.py:674-768` is fail-closed on the chart radius and never rescales M, its inverse is the float64 inverse of the represented forward factor, and the sqrt(d) r bound on ||A||_1 and ||A||_inf is valid. `fold_rope_into_frame` at `vfe3/geometry/transport.py:250-299` gives V_i = U_i R_i^T with stored inverse R_i U_i^{-1}, so Omega_ij = U_i R(theta_j - theta_i) U_j^{-1}, and the compact slicing matches the per-head blocks. `compute_transport_operators` at `vfe3/geometry/transport.py:946-951` overwrites Omega_ii with I, and its derivative there is zero. `rope_commutant_projection` at `vfe3/geometry/rope.py:119-156` is the Frobenius-orthogonal projection onto the torus commutant (orthogonality residual 7e-15), with R_i P R_i^T = P to 1.8e-15 at fractions 1 and 0.5 and P SPD. For `comparison_invariant` at `vfe3/gradients/relational.py:149-197`, the KL of (R_i a, P(G_q)) against (R_{i+5} b, P(G_k)) was identical across i to machine precision (0.5761082807014208), and the non-invariant comparison mode is also exactly relative-position (46.0854434458 +/- 4e-13). `logm_principal` at `vfe3/geometry/matrix_log.py:109-149` matches scipy logm to 4e-15 to 9e-15 at d up to 8, log(exp X) recovers X near |Im| = 3, and diag(-1, -2) fails closed with NaN, consistent with Culver 1966. `logm_frechet_pair` at `vfe3/geometry/matrix_log.py:209-272` matches a central finite difference to 1.5e-10 to 1.7e-9. The pullback direction at `vfe3/geometry/phi_preconditioner.py:812-967` solves (J^T J + 1e-6 B) v = grad for J_a = dexp_phi(G_a) and agrees with a finite-difference metric solve to 1.1e-9 in both series and spectral modes, and xi = Psi_L(ad_phi) v gives exp(phi - eps v) = exp(phi) exp(-eps xi) to O(eps^2), with 2.1e-4 at eps = 1e-2 and 1.9e-6 at eps = 1e-3. The series tail majorant at `vfe3/geometry/phi_preconditioner.py:315-401` is correct (first omitted term alpha^{k+1}/(k+2)!, ratio alpha/(order+2)). The U-space helpers at `vfe3/geometry/phi_preconditioner.py:1301-1422` give ambient-gradient recovery error 3.4e-10, chart velocity error 1.2e-10 on the strip and 1.5e-10 off it (eigenbasis), and a closed-form xi = U^{-1} G_bar that equals the series xi up to the 1e-6 ridge (1.6e-5). The pullback metric is labeled at `vfe3/geometry/phi_preconditioner.py:1-17` as the Frobenius pullback through d exp rather than Fisher, and that label is correct. The completed operator C^{-1} X (c1 C + xi xi^T) + c2 X^T at `vfe3/geometry/phi_fisher.py:64-169` equals the pulled-back Gaussian Fisher-Rao form plus lambda ||asym Z||^2 (quadratic form error up to 2.6e-16), `fisher_spectrum_bounds` matches the dense generalized eigenvalues exactly for lambda in {0.5, 1, 2, 3}, `fisher_type_solve` matches a dense solve to 1.5e-15, and the metric is PD for lambda > 0 since 2 lambda + c1 a > 0. `pooled_pos_operator` at `vfe3/geometry/phi_fisher.py:187-211` equals `completed_fisher_operator` at B = 1 to 2.9e-15. In the chart retraction staging at `vfe3/gauge_optim.py:374-558` the product residual is at most 6.5e-7 after backtracking, and the descent gate and per-row rejection were read. The group_log staging at `vfe3/gauge_optim.py:851-996` is the exact right product with residual 3e-15, with the trust radius on the body generator norm. `bound_phi_frame_condition_` at `vfe3/gauge_optim.py:1155-1413` brought max kappa_2 from 2447 to 48.6 against kappa_max = 50, preserving the skew part (4e-16) and the trace (4e-15) and never increasing ||X||_F, and the log-norm bound and Frobenius-nearest projection hold. In `vfe3/model/edge_links.py:50-215` the identity exp(AB^T) = I + A phi1(B^T A) B^T holds to 5e-16, `edge_reference_message` equals the explicit sum of G_ij Pi(beta_ij) xi_j to 3.4e-16 (lowrank) and 7.5e-16 (pure_gauge), and the pure_gauge triangle holonomy has trace equal to tr F to 3.6e-15. `loop_energy` at `vfe3/model/edge_links.py:633-702` matches the explicit 0.5 sum w ||W_ijk - I||_F^2 to 7e-16 (no window) and 2e-15 (window 2), with the loop index set following i - w <= k < j <= i. At `vfe3/model/relational_operators.py:31-46,210-228`, phi1 is computed through the augmented exponential, O' = e^{OT} T^{-1} = T^{-1} + O phi1(TO), and the effective transfer is T O' = e^{TO} in GL+. `spd_geodesic_distance` at `vfe3/metrics.py:720-758` is the AIRM distance ||log(A^{-1/2} B A^{-1/2})||_F (Bhatia's ||.||_2 is the Frobenius norm), computed through Cholesky generalized eigenvalues. The claims for the new block_sl, block_borel and block_unipotent groups at `vfe3/geometry/groups.py:989-1075` hold: exp is not onto SL(2) (diag(-2, -1/2)), b+(d) and n+(d) are exponential groups because their ad spectra are real, and Iwasawa gives GL+ = SO(d) B+(d). In `vfe3/table_optim.py:1-31,134-209` the diagonal-Gaussian Fisher metric in (mu, log s) is diag(1/s, 1/2), so the natural step is (N/n_v)(s * g_mu, 2 g_l) with s = exp(l) on the normal range. `apply_mu_trust_region` at `vfe3/numerics.py:164-187` is a Mahalanobis ball, which is GL-covariant. The diagonal LayerNorm pushforward at `vfe3/model/block_mlp.py:367-386` equals diag(J diag(sigma) J^T) with J_kl = (gamma_k/s)(delta_kl - (1 + z_k z_l)/K), plus the floor and radial terms.

### numerics

The `_GramPrecision` closed-form backward at `vfe3/relational_scoring.py:422-477` passes float64 gradcheck on the dense Gram route of `projected_gaussian_kl` (B = 1, N = 3, H = 2, r = 4), and the diagonal route at `vfe3/relational_scoring.py:1322-1406` also passes float64 gradcheck. With `query_row_constant_grad=False` at `vfe3/relational_scoring.py:988-995`, gradcheck fails only through the detached query log-determinant, which is the documented row-constant behavior. For the unshifted `cholesky_qr2` at `vfe3/numerics.py:620-667`, the fp32 forward precision error and autograd gradient error are O(u kappa) (gradient 6.3e-7 to 1.2e-4 at kappa 1e1 to 3e3) against the Gram route's O(u kappa^2) (2.9e-6 to 9.6e-2), so the factor route's accuracy claim holds for gradients too. In `triangular_condition_estimate` at `vfe3/numerics.py:670-704`, the Hager/Higham iteration and the alternating vector with its 2/(3n) normalization are correct, and on random CholeskyQR2 factors (r = 32) the estimate was at least 2.3 kappa_2. `FACTOR_CONDITION_TOL=3e-4` at `vfe3/numerics.py:601` (kappa_1 hat > 2516) sits below CholeskyQR2's measured fp32 failure onset (48% failures at kappa 1e4, none at 3e3). The Daleckii-Krein adjoint of min(lambda, c) at `vfe3/model/covariance_ceiling.py:57-78` passes gradcheck, degenerate eigenvalues match finite differences to 6.6e-11, and F_ab is clamped to [0, 1]. In `safe_kl_clamp` at `vfe3/families/base.py:54-62`, a NaN or inf input receives zero gradient through the clamp and the `nan_to_num` backward. The `x_minus_log1p` series coefficients and the `ratio_minus_one_minus_log` branch isolation at `vfe3/families/base.py:21-51` are correct, with unselected branches seeing placeholder inputs. `bounded_variance_from_log` at `vfe3/numerics.py:124-146` uses max_log = 80, which is inside fp32 range (e^80 = 5.5e34) and inert at the live sigma_log of 1.61. The `apply_mu_trust_region` sentinel at `vfe3/numerics.py:214-216` is sized so that K squared sentinels do not overflow. In `validated_cholesky_solve` at `vfe3/numerics.py:472-594`, the residual tolerance 64 K eps is above the Higham Thm 10.3 Cholesky backward-error bound (K+1)u, and the condition limit is consistent. `safe_cholesky` at `vfe3/numerics.py:314-367` chooses the ridge without grad, keeps the final factor differentiable and has failed rows read the identity, and the absolute ridge for mean diagonal < 1 is documented. The diagonal Renyi float64 island inside |alpha-1| < 1e-2 and the alpha > 1 nonpositive-blend mask at `vfe3/families/gaussian.py:289-348` are correct. The fp64 escalation at `vfe3/model/relational_operators.py:1265-1319` recomputes only failing sequences and splits a per-sequence frame correctly. The centered spread at `vfe3/model/relational_operators.py:54-95` is accumulated about each row's routed mean, so it carries no offset cancellation. The relative floor at `vfe3/model/relational_operators.py:941-976` is positive by construction (diag(U U^T) is a sum of squares) under any matmul precision.

### variational

In `free_energy` at `vfe3/free_energy.py:254-336`, the beta block equals -tau log Z to 1.4e-14 (ALiBi prior, per-head tau), including a finite prior 200 nats deep in the tail, and dF/dE = lambda_beta*beta to 2.8e-16 at lambda_beta 1 and 0.37, with the same factor on coupling and entropy and none inside the softmax. At `vfe3/free_energy.py:314-333`, pi is log_softmax(B) with -inf entries neutralized, xlogy is zero-safe at beta = 0 without flooring positive mass, and the entropy uses the same broadcast tau as the softmax. The surrogate gradient identity d/dE[sum beta E] = beta - tau^-1 beta (E - E_beta[E]) holds with residual 1.1e-16 (executed; the include_attention_entropy toggle is retired at `config.py:2584`). In `canonical_routing_rows` at `vfe3/free_energy.py:535-657`, belief row plus entropy row plus tau*logZ equals 0 to 4.4e-16, the head KL matches a dense float64 KL to 8.9e-16, and `tests/test_canonical_free_energy_20261010.py` passes. The canonical diagnostic at `vfe3/model/model.py:2061-2168` runs on the tiny live route (comparison_invariant RoPE, causal_alibi, block_glk), and total = self + lambda_beta(belief + entropy) holds exactly per layer. `full_link_gradients` at `vfe3/gradients/full_link.py:526-554` equals autograd of the profiled cost c0 log(b0+D) + G to at most 1.8e-15 on the diagonal, diagonal per-coordinate and full families. The closed form at rho = 1 at `vfe3/gradients/full_link.py:604-671` is a descent (MM) step on the profiled energy on both families, the full-family damped update is the exact natural-gradient flow theta(t) = (1-rho)theta0 + rho theta*, rho = 1-exp(-ct) (derived), and the `full_link_cost_parts` deltas equal the frozen-cost deltas. The state returned by `closed_form_profiled` at `vfe3/gradients/full_link.py:749-902` is stationary for the profiled energy (|grad mu| 8.9e-16 and |grad sigma| 4.9e-15 diagonal; 1.4e-15 and 2.2e-15 full), the `_DivergenceCurve` D_mu and D_Sigma formulas were re-derived, per-coordinate alpha is refused at `config.py:984`, and `tests/test_closed_form_profiled.py` passes. For the channel-likelihood context at `vfe3/gradients/full_link.py:401-522`, Lambda = U^-T sum_h P_h^T W_h P_h U^-1 + alpha P_p and eta were re-derived from E_q[-log N(P xi; v, R)], and the entropy is counted once (c = alpha), so the minimizer is the tempered posterior as the README claims. In `vfe3/alpha_i.py`, alpha* = c0/(b0+D) is the stationary point of alpha D + b0 alpha - c0 log alpha with profiled value c0 log(b0+D) + const, and list-valued b0/c0 are refused outside per-coordinate mode (`config.py:1385-1405`). The Press slopes at `vfe3/attention_prior.py:23-47` are correct, including non-power-of-two H = 5 ([1/4, 1/16, 1/64, 1/256, 1/2] at base 1), and the log-prior bias is normalized consistently wherever pi enters an entropy. The mean-field label step in `vfe3/model/minimal_agent.py` is the exact CAVI update (E_q log N(SX;b,B) = -KL - d/2(1+log 2pi) - 1/2 logdet A), and the ELBO was non-decreasing over 6 sweeps (minimum per-receiver increment 0.0) and below the importance-sampled evidence (executed, coupled heads, ALiBi prior). The ELBO and importance-sampled evidence at `vfe3/model/minimal_agent.py:1012-1200` were checked term by term, with entropy and normalizer terms each counted once and the RoPE rotation handled. The mean-field power posterior and `prototype_elbo` at `vfe3/model/prototype_agents.py:584-755` give an ELBO non-decreasing over 6 sweeps and below the exact evidence (largest ELBO minus evidence -1.7e-2). The exact EM objective at `vfe3/model/prototype_agents.py:519-580` with `block_mlp.py:1477-1523` has gradient equal to grad log p(xi|theta) to at most 6.7e-16 on the diagonal, dense and block layouts (Fisher identity), and the EM gradient reaches only nu, log_lambda and log_pi on the live route (executed). The moment-matched kernel covariance at `vfe3/model/relational_operators.py:1198-1263` agrees term by term with item 2 of Theorem thm:probabilistic_status (label/source independence, cross-head coincidence term c^{hh'} = beta^h beta^{h'}). Under the row_min clamp at `vfe3/gradients/relational.py:221-253`, the query log-det row constant cancels exactly in the gap, so skipping its autograd is exact, and the lower clamp at 0 keeps D >= 0 as item 1 of the Theorem requires. In `vfe3/inference/e_step.py`, the predictive route requires n_e_steps = 1 (`config.py:975`), the full-link episode freezes routing and sources and refreshes alpha each iteration as documented, and `tests/test_e_step.py` and `tests/test_free_energy.py` pass. For the observation term, `free_energy(log_likelihood=)` has no production caller and the decoder CE is the only live data term, so there is no double counting. Target blindness holds: targets enter the EM term only as the scored-position mask (`model.py:1393-1396`), not the beliefs.

### info-geometry

The diagonal Rényi/KL closed form at `vfe3/families/gaussian.py:289-348` matches 1-D quadrature of the Rényi definition to 1e-16 at α = 0.3, 0.5, 0.8 and 1.5, with the blend (1-α)σ_q + ασ_t and 1/(α-1) placed correctly. The diagonal KL slot orientation at `vfe3/families/gaussian.py:124-160` is correct: the code's KL(q||t) equals the quadrature of E_q[log q - log t] (0.312422309792 both). The α→1 continuity at `vfe3/families/gaussian.py:316-345` gives relative error 1e-8 to 5e-8 in fp32 at α = 1±1e-3, 1-1e-5 and 1+2e-6, and 1e-12 to 1e-14 in fp64; the float64 band island at |α-1| < 1e-2 is reached in fp32, and the 1e-6 switch adds about 4e-6 relative, as designed. The full-covariance Rényi/KL at `vfe3/families/gaussian.py:641-788` has fp64 relative error 2e-16 to 2e-13 against an mpmath reference at α = 0.5, 0.9, 0.999, 1 and 1.001 and fp32 relative error at most 1.6e-7, and a non-PD blend at α = 1.5 returns kl_max. The generic log-partition Rényi/Bregman KL at `vfe3/families/base.py:482-531` equals the closed forms (full and diagonal, α = 0.5, 1, 0.999, 1.5). The full per-coordinate conditional-KL chain at `vfe3/families/gaussian.py:99-121` sums to KL within 1e-15. The squared Hellinger 1-BC and Bhattacharyya -log BC at `vfe3/families/base.py:574-621` equal 1-D quadrature to 1e-16, and self-divergences are exactly 0. `safe_kl_clamp` at `vfe3/families/base.py:54-62` with kl_max = inf maps NaN to +inf, so the non-PD α > 1 blend has a pure (+inf) path, and Rényi is not reachable in training because divergence_family/renyi_order are retired and pinned at renyi/1.0 (`config.py:2584`). In `vfe3/families/base.py:332-467` and `vfe3/alpha_i.py:18-37`, duplicate registry keys fail closed, squared_hellinger has no per-coordinate form, and `free_energy.py:144-155` rejects it. The exponential-family bookkeeping at `vfe3/families/gaussian.py:163-287` (natural parameters, A(θ), E[T] and entropy) is mutually consistent for the diagonal and full families. `projected_gaussian_kl` at `vfe3/relational_scoring.py:936-1491` computes KL(q_i||k_j) with the key in the second slot, matching a dense float64 reference to 4e-14 and differing from the swapped orientation by 28.6; the GEMM expansion and diagonal route are exact, the fp32 error is 4.8e-6 relative, float64 gradcheck passes, and `query_row_constant_grad=False` changes gradients only by row constants (2e-16 on row-centered weights). noisy_query at `vfe3/relational_scoring.py:1494-1647` equals -log N(a_i; b_j, G_q+G_k) - ½ log det(2π G_q) (log_prob check 1.3383504157), is nonnegative and passes gradcheck, and the README restricts the exact-posterior claim to τ = 1. noisy_key at `vfe3/relational_scoring.py:1650-1692` equals KL against G_k + n_h I (7e-15), the KL(Q_{i,β}||P_i) joint identity holds, and it routes at τ = 1 (`relational_operators.py:1627-1628`). mean_distance at `vfe3/relational_scoring.py:1722-1773` equals ½||a_i - b_j||² (4e-14). In `vfe3/model/prior_bank.py:1386-1532` and `vfe3/kernels/kl_decode.py`, decode logits are -KL(q_i||p_v)/τ_eff + κ log π_v, the CE matches a direct reference exactly (17.389292260236992), the add-one unigram prior is built from the training stream (`train.py:4346-4357`), and the Triton forward and backward formulas (G, dper_pos, dτ) agree with the analytic derivative. The expected-likelihood decode -½Σ[d²/s + log s]/τ in `vfe3/model/prior_bank.py:1942-2052` and `vfe3/kernels/expected_likelihood.py` matches its reference to 1.8e-15, and the analytic Triton backward formulas are correct. The `decode_second_order` delta-method term ½ tr(Σ Cov_p(w)) at `vfe3/model/prior_bank.py:1770-1940` is correct. For the table Fisher step at `vfe3/table_optim.py:134-209`, G_v = diag(1/s_v, 1/2) is the Fisher of N(μ, diag e^l) in (μ, l), and the (N/n_v) count weighting, the trust radius in Fisher norm, the decay order and the schedule factor match the formula in an executed step (6e-8); `config.py:1932-1942` rejects decoders that read the prior tables. For the completed frame-Fisher operator at `vfe3/geometry/phi_fisher.py:69-169`, the λ = 0 pullback equals 2KL/t² by finite differences, the closed-form and Woodbury solves have residual 1e-16, `fisher_spectrum_bounds` equals the dense generalized spectrum at λ = 0.7, 1, 2 and 3, and the operator is symmetric. The Gaussian natural gradient at `vfe3/geometry/retraction.py:774-815` (Σ∇μ, 2Σ∇ΣΣ symmetrized; diagonal 2σ²∇σ) is correct with the rank dispatch right for the shapes used, and the partials at `vfe3/gradients/full_link.py:526-554` give the Fisher flow Ċ = cC - CΛC. The profiled self-coupling at `vfe3/gradients/full_link.py:112-141` uses the unclamped KL(q||p), so α* = c0/(b0+D) does not collapse at kl_max, and the stationarity and envelope in `alpha_i.py` are correct. The canonical routing diagnostic at `vfe3/free_energy.py:535-645` uses KL(N(ξ_i,C_i)||N(ξ_j,C_j)) with the key in the second slot. The router saturation metric at `vfe3/model/model.py:1705-1745` reads the post-clamp (row_min-shifted) captured cost, so klmax_frac measures the binding clamp under both references. The AIRM distance at `vfe3/metrics.py:720-758` is explicitly not labeled Fisher-Rao (the factor ½ is noted), and half_fisher_trace is correct. The pullback conditioner at `vfe3/geometry/phi_preconditioner.py:12-16` is explicitly not called a natural gradient, and no Amari/Rényi α conflation or Chentsov miscitation was found in `vfe3/` or `README.md`. In the reference decode at `vfe3/model/prior_bank.py:1086-1103`, replacing C by diag C shifts KL by the per-position constant ½(log det diag C - log det C), so the softmax is exact. The head-block projection at `vfe3/families/gaussian.py:791-803` is the M-projection argmin_q KL(p||q) over block-diagonal q.

### attention

Causality holds bitwise, with maximum past-logit and past-loss change 0.0 on both the inference and training-loss paths, across 50 route variants in `vfe3/model/model.py:945-1408`: the live route; row_min; causal, causal_noself, causal_alibi, causal_alibi_noself and causal_windowed (w = 2); rope frame, frame_value, comparison and comparison_invariant; learned pos_phi; attention_residual; passthrough covariance; noisy_query, noisy_key and mean_distance; gaussian_full and gaussian_block; minimal_agent mean_field, exact and spread_corrected; projected_gradient closed_form, closed_form_profiled and euler; block MLP coordinate, prototype_agents (posterior, exact), canonical_frame, gaussian_template, gauge_gate and delta_full; block and decode reference coordinates; the mahalanobis norm; content_link_edges lowrank, latent+loop and pure_gauge; decode full_chunked, expected_likelihood_chunked and linear; learned temperature; tie_comparison_maps; per_type_hidden_bias; and prior_handoff_sigma = 0.5. Every softmax, log_softmax and logsumexp in the attention path reduces over the key axis dim=-1 (`vfe3/free_energy.py:201,222,226`; `vfe3/model/minimal_agent.py:507,594`; `vfe3/model/edge_links.py:612`). The mask is an additive -inf in the logits before the softmax, masked beta entries are exactly 0.0, rows sum to 1 within 1.2e-7, and every registered prior keeps the diagonal or (0,0), so no row is fully masked (`vfe3/attention_prior.py:121-248`; `vfe3/free_energy.py:186-201`). With per-head temperature, beta equals an explicit per-head softmax loop with tau_h = kappa_h*sqrt(d_h) bitwise for block_glk, mixed so_n [3,5] and list kappa (`vfe3/free_energy.py:31-92`; `vfe3/model/relational_operators.py:1604-1634`). The routing score equals an explicit float64 KL(query_i || key_j) oracle within 5e-13 for rope none, comparison and comparison_invariant, with and without condition recovery, in layout [b,h,i,j] (`vfe3/gradients/relational.py:31-218`). Comparison RoPE rotates after the learned maps (relative positions), P(G) is the torus-commutant projection, and frame RoPE modes are refused with reference block or decode coordinates (`vfe3/geometry/rope.py:66-181`; `vfe3/config.py:1852-1894`). Decode logits equal -KL(q||p_v)/tau_eff + kappa log pi_v within 2.8e-14 in float64, and the unigram bias is normalized with logsumexp 0 (`vfe3/model/prior_bank.py:2180-2244, 1206-1227`). The fused chunked CE (chunk = 5, ignored targets) matches dense `F.cross_entropy` for diagonal fp32/fp64 a_v, reference coordinates, gaussian_block, full_chunked, expected_likelihood, linear and decode_covariance encoded/constant, with CE difference at most 2.9e-6 and maximum relative gradient difference at most 6.4e-6 (`vfe3/model/prior_bank.py:1386-1533, 1942-2052`). Reading the Triton KL decode kernels (`vfe3/kernels/_kl_decode_triton.py:33-123`; `vfe3/kernels/kl_decode.py:81-208`), the online LSE with an -inf-safe reference, the chunk-local target hit masked by mj, G = -0.5[c>=0](p-onehot)g/tau, dper_pos = -sum G, dtau = sum w kl/tau^2, dlhs = G rhs and [drhs, db] = G^T[lhs, 1] are correct, and G aliasing A is safe because each program owns its rows. Reading the Triton expected-likelihood kernels (`vfe3/kernels/_expected_likelihood_triton.py:28-195`; `vfe3/kernels/expected_likelihood.py:79-190`), E, the logits -cE+u and the gradients dmu_q = -2c sum w r, dsigma_q = c sum w(r^2-1/s), dmu_v = 2c sum w r and dtau = c/tau sum wE match the torch reference algebra. ALiBi slopes reproduce the Press et al. reference schedule, including non-power-of-two H (`vfe3/attention_prior.py:23-47`), and the alibi head-count guard is at construction (`vfe3/model/model.py:348-357`). The log-prior and RoPE caches key on every builder input and are cleared on `_apply`, and training after an inference_mode forward works for every rope_mode (`vfe3/model/model.py:595-667`). In the moment-matched covariance at `vfe3/model/relational_operators.py:1364-1568`, the head-pair coefficients beta^h beta^g off the diagonal and beta^h on it, the per-head spread and the residual cross term sum_h beta_ii M_h C_i all check algebraically, and the blockwise reshapes and permutes are consistent with the dense route. The predictive-content einsums align the head at -3 for beta and -2 for values, and the compact-frame reshape matches the head-major coordinate layout (`vfe3/model/relational_operators.py:764-805, 1677-1697`). Prior-handoff omission is exact on predictive stacks: no consumer reads mu_p, and sigma_p is kept when prior_handoff_sigma > 0 with a moment-matched readout (`vfe3/model/stack.py:35-59, 164-166`). In generation, the top-p unsort scatter is correct, truncating the context keeps relative RoPE/ALiBi positions, and the decode_last frame slice is correct (`vfe3/model/model.py:1411-1530, 817-889`). Expected-likelihood decode with gaussian_full or gaussian_block is refused by validation (the family/decode rank checks in `vfe3/config.py`, executed). The focused tests `tests/test_live_route_profile.py`, `tests/test_attention_prior.py`, `tests/test_rope_comparison_covariance_20261009.py` and `tests/test_chunked_decode.py` pass.

### training

On `live_config`, the group table of `build_optimizer` at `vfe3/train.py:212-461` prints every trainable parameter in exactly one group with the intended lr, weight_decay and role (tables 0, phi 0.1, block MLP 0.01, relational and LayerNorm 0), and the exact-coverage assertion is present. `m_phi_group_trust_radius` and `m_phi_group_retraction` are now marked inactive under adamw at `vfe3/config.py:2088-2091`, and `train_vfe3.py:565-569` lists them, so the brief's known instance is fixed. The warmup/cosine/floor schedule at `vfe3/train.py:524-564` was executed: the floor is 0.01, a base-0 group stays exactly 0, the peak is reached at step 100 and the end multiplier is 0.01. Resume at `vfe3/train.py:4329-4337` rebuilds LambdaLR at last_epoch = successful_updates-1, and the reconstructed LRs equal the uninterrupted schedule at k = 1, 100 and 250 (executed). The scheduler clocks only on accepted updates (`vfe3/train.py:1250-1253`). Resume is exact under the fisher table route: 4 straight steps against 2 steps plus checkpoint plus resume gave max |diff| = 0.0 over all parameters (executed, `live_config` tiny). The sliding-window eval at `vfe3/data/datasets.py:830-853` scores every transition exactly once with at least N-s+1 context, over T in [6, 60), L in {4, 8, 16} and every stride (executed, 0 violations). The grouped trailing-padding truncated forward at `vfe3/train.py:1413-1440` equals the full padded forward (CE 1.93621826 against 1.93621838, 6 scored; executed). PPL at `vfe3/metric_contracts.py:21-27` is exact exp(CE) with overflow to inf, and the exp(min(ce,20)) saturation named in the brief no longer exists. `evaluate` at `vfe3/train.py:1359-1473` is no_grad, runs in eval mode, restores the training flag and uses token-weighted CE with exact int64 target accounting. The generalization-gap train side at `vfe3/train.py:1477-1510` scores the same tail positions as stride-32 validation, under `fork_rng`. In `vfe3/table_optim.py:150-193`, the Fisher metric diag(1/s, 1/2) and the natural direction (s g_mu, 2 g_l) are correct for s = e^l, rows with n_v = 0 take no step, decay is applied outside the trust bound, the fused GradScaler grad_scale/found_inf contract is honored, and `tests/test_table_fisher_20261010.py` passes (9). Fisher mode refuses decoders that read the prior tables (`vfe3/config.py:1932-1942`), and the tables are gathered only at input tokens (`vfe3/model/prior_bank.py:2124-2154`), so input-token counts are the right n_v. In `GaugeManifoldAdamW` at `vfe3/gauge_optim.py:1443-1822`, the lr = 0 path leaves phi unchanged, the chart step has derivative -v_phi and group_log uses the exact product, and `tests/test_gauge_optim.py` passes (22). Grad-accumulation normalization at `vfe3/train.py:956-964` happens once, after unscale, by the total scored-token count. The nonfinite gate at `vfe3/train.py:1129-1166` zeroes gradients and takes no AdamW or table step, and the scaler backoff path is distinguished from a nonfinite loss. The launcher's inert E-step settings (e_q_mu_lr, e_sigma_q_trust, and others) are correctly marked inactive on the active route at `vfe3/config.py:2092-2118` (executed). `seed_everything` and the CUBLAS workspace handling at `vfe3/runtime.py:90-110` are consistent across multi-seed launches.

### config-wiring

All 122 launcher keys in `train_vfe3.py:103-353` are `VFE3Config` fields with none unknown, and `__post_init__` leaves every launcher value unchanged (only kl_max None -> 1280.0 = 8K, and no other write fires on the live dict). With `inactive_fields` on the live config (`vfe3/config.py:2069-2426`), 29 marked-inert launcher knobs were perturbed and every one was bitwise identical in loss and all parameters after 3 tiny train steps; unmarked knobs either changed the run (kappa_beta, alibi_slope, eps, grad_clip_scope, floor_mode, routing_kl_max_reference, decode_chunk_size, decode_av_precision, prior_handoff_sigma) or are rare-trigger guards or memory toggles. The exact bool/int type gate at `vfe3/config.py:879-886` runs before validation, since field types are real objects with no postponed annotations. `config_from_serialized` at `vfe3/config.py:3090-3187` raises on unknown keys (strict by default), and the lenient loader at `vfe3/legacy_config.py:24-36` drops unknown keys but returns them and hands retired keys through. The serialize/reload round trip and semantic fingerprint equality at `vfe3/config.py:2569-2578` hold for live, so_n random, so_n list-of-lists, su_n shared and per_head, block_sl, block_borel, kl_max = inf, frozen and off. Every other default flip of a field introduced after 2026-09-23 is pinned at `vfe3/config.py:3045-3055` (content_link_chart, predictive_covariance, m_decode_mu_lr/sigma_lr, relational_receiver_measurement, decode_logits_amp, decode_ce_triton), and minimal_agent_inference was co-introduced with predictive_readout, so it needs none. On the pure path at `vfe3/gauge_covariant.py:33-95`, overrides from the live launcher give `gauge_covariance_failures == []`, the model trains at tiny scale, and at launcher width (K = 160, H = 5, L = 4, N = 16) 20 of 20 first forwards and 12 training steps were finite and decreasing. `tests/profiles.py`, `tests/test_config_isolation.py` and `tests/test_live_route_profile.py` pass on the working tree (13 tests), and `_CONFIG_VALUES` covers every field. Random sweeps of about 5,100 accepted configs over families, decoders, readouts, MLP modes, norms, coordinates, rope modes, groups, tyings, phi and table optimizers and links built and ran every accepted config, except the gaussian_full+LN+pushforward numerical failures reported. `grad_clip_scope` reaches `train_step` from cfg at `vfe3/train.py:4734`, and `build_optimizer` at `vfe3/train.py:212-461` resolves every None-inherited rate through `resolved_hyperparameter`. A grep found no post-construction mutation of `model.cfg` in `vfe3/`, and the `dataclasses.replace` uses at `vfe3/posthoc/evaluate.py:116` and `vfe3/semantics/run.py:400` re-run `__post_init__`. `deferred_host_checks` and `host_read` at `vfe3/contracts.py:16-74` resolve queued verdicts in registration order before the caller's own values. The overflow and finiteness handling of `perplexity_from_ce` and `perplexity_matches_ce` in `vfe3/metric_contracts.py` is correct. The literal-versus-resolved table in `vfe3/config_markdown.py` produces no spurious rows for equal int/float literals (sigma_init = 5, relational_key_noise = 1). The phi_weight_decay override by policy metadata at `vfe3/config.py:2020-2035` is warned, so the recorded-versus-used divergence is disclosed.

### experiments

`_student_t_quantile_975` at `multiseed_analysis.py:203-239` matches t_{0.975,nu} to under 4e-8 for nu = 1..29 (executed), and the t-quantile and noise-floor cases of `tests/test_audit_20261012_analysis.py` pass under CPU python with scipy 1.17.1. The noise floor t(n-1, 0.975)*sqrt(2)*s at `multiseed_analysis.py:189-200` is the correct half-width for a difference of two single-seed runs (6.08 s at n = 3). The homogeneity fingerprint at `multiseed_analysis.py:85-178` strips only seed keys and normalizes int/float, and data_seed is reported rather than used as a comparability key. The caveat about shared data order at `multiseed_analysis.py:1017-1018,1115` is carried into the digest. In `vfe3/baseline.py:689-728,813-932`, perturbing token 5 leaves logits at positions < 5 exactly unchanged for plain, rope, block_rw phi_like, post_shared without residual and the causal prior (executed). The decoder is untied from tok_embed, and a zero-init block_read equals the plain baseline bitwise at the same seed (`vfe3/baseline.py:633-686,833`, executed). The analytic context and total parameter counts at `vfe3/baseline.py:335-353` equal the measured counts (1152 and 1691 on the tiny probe). Every trainable parameter lands in exactly one AdamW group at `vfe3/baseline.py:1108-1164`, and hidden_lr_scale applies only to `blocks.*` matrices. The top-k and nucleus masks at `vfe3/model/model.py:1487-1528` are correct: nucleus keeps the top token and the scatter un-sort is a valid inverse permutation. `stream_ce` at `generate_text.py:271-325` scores each tail token exactly once with at least seq_len-stride+1 tokens of context. The train loader memo key at `ablation.py:1878-1926,2263-2276` includes eval_stride, and the generator is re-pinned per cell to DATA_SEED (or cfg.seed) after model construction. `validate_sweeps` at `ablation.py:1580-1735` refuses a swept contrast that is inert on every arm. An eval_stride above max_seq_len is rewritten to disjoint windows with a warning and recorded in the result (`ablation.py:1933-1960`). `run_cell` at `scaling.py:1250-1354` reports the realized parameter count and re-pins the train generator per cell, and resume compares the migrated effective config, data identity and code identity. The lack-of-fit F statistic and dof at `scaling_analysis.py:954-1020` follow Draper and Smith, and at `scaling_analysis.py:1098-1129` identical runs are counted once in the pooled fit, LOF and bootstrap. The leaderboard at `parameter_optimization.py:861-909` ranks only candidates with every requested seed. The decision rule at `vfe3/paper/tables.py:385-418,471-538` pairs on identical (seed, data_seed), and since the claim needs every clause to hold (intersection-union), no multiplicity correction is needed. `run_vfe` at `vfe3/experiment.py:122-221` reseeds before and after model construction, seeds the shuffle from data_seed only and clears an inherited resume_from. `check_base_data` at `train_baseline.py:140-190` refuses dataset, tokenizer, max_tokens or data-seed mismatches against the base provenance.

### posthoc-paper

In `nll_pass` at `vfe3/posthoc/evaluate.py:194-270`, target_pos = start + col + 1 and context = col + 1 are correct for `TokenWindows` and `ScoredTailWindows`, and on the tiny live route the scored targets are exactly 1..T-1, once each (44 unique of 44). The cross-check at `vfe3/posthoc/evaluate.py:273-292` against `vfe3.train.evaluate` on the tiny live route (CPU, fp32) gives delta CE -1.6e-8 with the padded final window included and equal scored counts. The bootstrap at `vfe3/posthoc/evaluate.py:299-373` is correct in its ratio-of-sums article bootstrap and paired delta (a constant +0.01 shift gives point/lo/hi = 0.01 exactly), and the shared seed pairs rows by design. All arms in `vfe3/posthoc/interventions.py` restore the model exactly (re-run max |delta nll| = 0.0 after every arm), and rows 2 and 3 intercept exactly n_batches x n_layers routing softmaxes (8 = 4 x 2). The statistic definitions at `vfe3/posthoc/attention.py:66-144` match the training probe (`vfe3/train.py:2702-2734`), and the row-to-article mapping and the (H, B*N) flattening order agree. The at-cap test at `vfe3/posthoc/klcap.py:71-126` on the clamped cost matches `clamped_routing_cost`, with kl_max taken from cfg.kl_max as in `vfe3/model/block.py:46`. `fixed_train_sample` at `vfe3/posthoc/streams.py:285-336` reproduces `vfe3/train.py:2510-2547` (same candidates, seed and ceil rule; the training sample is a prefix of the post-hoc draw). `article_ids` at `vfe3/posthoc/streams.py:369-420` maps a heading's first byte to its containing token correctly (searchsorted side='right' on exclusive ends). The Panel A components at `vfe3/posthoc/routing.py:300-367` sum exactly to -D/tau + B with the row constant, and the cap and row_min reference are handled. The relative least-squares scale c and the Frobenius and log-det gaps at `vfe3/posthoc/covariance.py:195-207` are algebraically correct, including off-block energy for full Sigma. The conserved-quantity drifts at `vfe3/posthoc/maps.py:271-381` are NaN under non-affine charts and comparison RoPE, and the content map uses the chart's effective O'. The ce_by_context SE formula and the CONTEXT_MIN_TARGETS filter for strided protocols at `vfe3/posthoc/figures.py:490-506` are correct. The word-count conventions and the target-coverage check at `vfe3/posthoc/calibration.py:128-140, 244-271` are correct, with duplicates and missing targets detected. For the reference keys at `vfe3/posthoc/report.py:176-202`, final_val_scored_targets is now written (`run_artifacts.py:3966-3968` with evaluate accounting), so the fix for item 7 of the 2026-10-12 audit holds. `select_checkpoint` at `vfe3/paper/runs.py:598-660` names each source and uses only full-split ok post-hoc passes, and `vfe3/paper/tables.py:385-418` pairs runs on (seed, data_seed) with a correct t-based CI and one-sided p. A grep found that every `vfe3/paper` summary, metrics and provenance key read by `tables.py` and `figures.py` has a writer in `train.py`/`run_artifacts.py`. Every analysis forward runs no-grad in eval mode: `nll_pass`, `protocol_evaluate` (evaluate is no_grad and sets eval), `measure_latency`, `routing._forward`, covariance (`@no_grad`) and feature_probes (eval plus no_grad), and `load_model` sets eval.

### semantics

The debiased linear CKA at `vfe3/semantics/stats.py:425-482` matches an explicit unbiased HSIC (Song et al.) on random data, 0.5616202 against 0.5616202 (executed). `_stratified_chance` at `vfe3/semantics/stats.py:650-670` equals the Monte Carlo stratified-null mean within 1 SE (0.25200 against 0.25180, SE 0.00037), and the permutation chance is 0.24972 against a null mean of 0.24936 (executed). The within-stratum label permutation at `vfe3/semantics/stats.py:730-739` (argsort/lexsort pairing) is a correct within-stratum shuffle (read). `unit_bootstrap_weights` at `vfe3/semantics/stats.py:584-611` equal c_u*c_v from explicit multiplicities (executed). IsoScore at `vfe3/semantics/stats.py:146-167` is 0.9995 for isotropic 8-d data and 0 for rank 1, and effective rank is 7.9996 for isotropic 8-d data (executed). TwoNN at `vfe3/semantics/stats.py:386-418` gives MLE 2.99 and fit 2.94 on a 3-d subspace in 10-d (executed). The Spearman percentile and cluster bootstrap at `vfe3/semantics/stats.py:489-546` are correct, and the grouped variant drops the iid p (read). The Wilson interval formula and exact endpoints at `vfe3/semantics/stats.py:563-581` are correct (read). The frequency probe at `vfe3/semantics/stats.py:814-868` uses KFold CV RidgeCV, a permuted-target control on the same folds and a target-free PCA, so there is no target leakage (read). In `vfe3/semantics/gaussians.py:89-284`, KL, one-vs-all KL, Jeffreys, Bhattacharyya (against a numerical integral), Fisher-Rao (against the Costa et al. closed form) and log distribution cosine all match references to 1e-15 (executed). The cluster bootstrap over hypernym groups and the paired KL-minus-frequency difference at `vfe3/semantics/gaussians.py:382-437` are correct (read). The KL-asymmetry entailment direction (specific density inside general) and its baselines at `vfe3/semantics/gaussians.py:612-675` are correct (read). The 3CosAdd, 3CosMul, PairDirection, ONLY-B and IGNORE-A formulas at `vfe3/semantics/vectors.py:282-334` are correct, and the Euclidean forms equal -||x-target||^2 up to per-question constants (read). The numpy and torch (TORCH_CPU) analogy ranks at `vfe3/semantics/vectors.py:340-409` are identical for all 5 methods, and 3CosAdd scores 1.0 against ONLY-B 0.0 on a planted Euclidean offset (executed). The word-pair resampling units, pair bootstrap, Wilson fallback and macro intervals at `vfe3/semantics/vectors.py:477-696` are correct (executed on a synthetic 8-pair category). OCS, LOO and top-PC from the offset Gram at `vfe3/semantics/vectors.py:741-853` are correct, as is the re-pairing Gram index algebra G_BB[pi,pi]-G_AB[:,pi].T-G_AB[:,pi]+G_AA (read). The causal whitening (pseudo-inverse sqrt on the range) and the frequency-matched replacement window, which never returns the target, at `vfe3/semantics/vectors.py:856-1001` are correct (read). The kNN numpy and torch paths at `vfe3/semantics/stats.py:239-286` agree on indices for cosine and Euclidean, with score differences of about 1e-7 (executed). `logm_real` at `vfe3/semantics/frames.py:272-316` reconstructs 50 random GL(4) elements to a 3.5e-15 residual (executed). The bi-invariant barycenter at `vfe3/semantics/frames.py:319-384` is equivariant, mean(gUh) = g mean(U) h to 1.6e-10 (executed). The gauge-fixed chart D0 e^X m^-1 D0^-1 at `vfe3/semantics/frames.py:431-515` is invariant under the split k and the diagonal rescaling D, with the trace identity tr L = tr X - log det m (read). At `vfe3/semantics/run.py:207-224`, the per_head block_glk coordinate layout reshape(n,H,d,d) equals the `group.embed_algebra` blocks exactly, and shared, block_so and block_sl via `embed_algebra` have zero off-block support (executed). The group analogy P = U_a* U_a^-1 U_b, the chart distance via inner products and the rerank residual log(P U_x^-1) at `vfe3/semantics/frames.py:704-829` are correct (read). The relation-transport T_ij = U_a*_j U_a_i^-1 orientation and the derangement null at `vfe3/semantics/frames.py:840-921` are correct (read). The positional pair-row relabeling index h(h-1)/2+l with inverse offset at `vfe3/semantics/frames.py:964-1007` is correct (read; inactive under pos_phi='none'). The routing covariance identity log det C = sum lv - 2 tr X at `vfe3/semantics/frames.py:1161-1226` is correct (read). The natural parameters eta = (m/t, -1/(2t))/tau at `vfe3/semantics/report.py:235-251` make the KL-decode logit linear in (mu, mu^2+Sigma) (read).
