### data-io

The `TokenWindows` eval contract at `vfe3/data/datasets.py:707-855` was swept over T < 40, L < 10 and s <= L with 0 violations of exactly-once scoring with x[p] == tok[t] and y[p] == tok[t+1]; per-epoch origins never produce a short window, and prefix masking does not mutate the corpus. The per-epoch origin bound b = min(s, T-L) and the window count at `vfe3/data/datasets.py:800-818` fit every origin (executed). `load_cached_tokens` at `vfe3/data/datasets.py:335-380` uses `torch.load(weights_only=True, mmap=True)`, validates dtype before slicing or casting, validates the .bin size and sidecar exactly, and returns owned int64 for capped loads. `cache_path` at `vfe3/data/datasets.py:92-116` validates dataset and split as safe path components, and `train_vfe3._run_label` and `experiment.run_vfe` validate dataset and label. `tokens_per_char` at `vfe3/data/datasets.py:634-704` was executed on the wikitext-103 validation split, where the decoded codepoints (1,136,998) equal the sidecar n_chars_source exactly. The split-aware loaders at `train_vfe3.py:404-436` shuffle and drop the tail only for train, with an explicit generator; val and test are sequential with pad_final and eval_stride; MAX_TOKENS caps train only; and a grep found no synthetic substitution anywhere in the launchers. `evaluate` at `vfe3/train.py:1360-1473` computes token-weighted CE over non -100 targets with the exact expected = scored + excluded partition, and the real run summary at 36.41 has test_scored_targets 280541 == test_stream_transitions 280541 and val n_windows 7654 matching the `TokenWindows` formula. The decode_unigram_prior counts at `vfe3/train.py:4346-4357` come from the train loader stream only, with no val/test leakage, and val and test touch training only through model selection. `load_checkpoint` at `vfe3/run_artifacts.py:2415-2772` uses weights_only=True with the weights_only=False fallback gated on trust_resume_checkpoint, stages on CPU, and checks data identity, cursor and step/epoch consistency before any live mutation. `save_checkpoint` at `vfe3/run_artifacts.py:2167-2275` carries the model, the optimizer with its parameter manifest, the CPU/CUDA RNG, the scaler, data_state including the epoch generator state and window_offset, the embedded validated best bundle, counters and wall clock, with an atomic publish. `_atomic_replace` and `_unique_sibling_temp` at `vfe3/run_artifacts.py:729-850` fsync before `os.replace`, retry on PermissionError and clean up temps with retries. A grep found that every `torch.load` call site under `vfe3/` and the root drivers uses weights_only=True, except the trust-gated fallback at `vfe3/run_artifacts.py:2493`. For `run_process_tree` at `vfe3/process_utils.py:370-505`, an executed argv round trip through the Windows gated launcher with spaces, quotes and trailing backslashes is exact, and a 2 s timeout killed the root and its grandchild (0 orphaned sleepers by a Win32_Process query). The source archive at `vfe3/run_archive.py:150-224` is written to a temp then `os.replace`d, with deterministic zip timestamps, and failures are recorded rather than raised. The vocabulary in `vfe3/data/word_vocab.py` is built from train-split counts only with a deterministic (-count, word) order, the save is atomic, and the load fails closed on schema mismatch. `build_wikitext103_word_cache.py:197-202` refuses before writing when caches exist without FORCE, and the vocabulary sidecar is published last. `start_step` and `finish_step` in `vfe3/timing.py:62-122` are always paired per step (`train.py:4724-4747`), so `sample_train_window` never divides by zero steps. `vfe3/path_utils.py:26-136` validates portable components, including Windows reserved stems, and the `vfe3/quiet.py` filter is scoped to two triton toolchain warnings. `vfe3/run_resources.py` uses metadata-only accounting with an mmap weights_only=True load. The JUnit accounting and exit codes of `check_junit.py`, `check_gpu_tests.py` and `run_cpu_tests.py` are correct, and `conftest.py` registers `tests.pytest_policy`. `tests/test_data.py` gives 14 passed under the CPU env. The finished-run `summary.json` files under `vfe3_runs/` parse as strict JSON with no stringified tensors (executed).

### viz-contracts

The figure_worker finalize request round trip is correct: `_run_figures_isolated` writes exact floats, bools and ints, the NaN floats survive json, and the validator accepts them (`vfe3/viz/figure_worker.py:74-113`; `vfe3/run_artifacts.py:947-957`). The metrics.csv string columns relational_semantics and relational_attention_capture survive `_history_from_csv` and are filtered by the isinstance gate in `_hist_subset`, and no `_aligned` key carries a string (`vfe3/run_artifacts.py:5362-5384`). On the tiny active route, the history dashboards have every GEOMETRY_HEALTH, VALIDATION_SANITY, OPTIMIZER_GEOMETRY and GRAD_NORM column written except toggle-gated ones (pos_phi, block_glmc, pullback, phi_mstep_max_matrix_norm), and all 12 history figures rendered with no failure warnings (`vfe3/viz/figures.py:1923-1932, 2898-2910, 3007-3014`; `vfe3/train.py:5130-5149`). The prior stale keys are fixed: final_val_scored_targets is now written and read (`vfe3/posthoc/report.py:183`; `vfe3/paper/tables.py:1014`), `_spearman_np` is replaced by the headline spearman_rho (`vfe3/viz/figures.py:5092`), and the bch_fidelity, dataset_revision and `__vfe3_config_schema__` readers are gone. The readout dashboard relational_* series are all present and finite on the active route (`vfe3/viz/figures.py:2738-2800`). The extract functions sigma_trace and belief_spectrum dispatch on family, so the diagonal (N,K) and full (N,K,K) layouts are handled, and the hard-coded diagonal=True in numerical_health is overridden by family= (`vfe3/metrics.py:396-413`; `vfe3/viz/extract.py:352, 532, 1183`). `BeliefState` now has only mu, sigma, phi and right_phi, and `_cpu_bank_value` preserves all four (`vfe3/belief.py:15-22`; `vfe3/viz/extract.py:66-72`). The report-bank decode equals the `model(tokens)` CE for decode_covariance belief and constant (`vfe3/viz/extract.py:217-249`). `plot_spd_ellipses` and `plot_belief_spectrum` handle covariance in a family-aware way (`vfe3/viz/figures.py:4089-4110, 4166-4202`). The sweep_adapters validation points are keyed on best_val_ppl only and labeled bits/token, not BPC (`vfe3/viz/sweep_adapters.py:44-120`). The paper resources and throughput readers match the resources.json schema (q50 fallback), and device_hours falls back to the summary wall_time_s as documented (`vfe3/paper/tables.py:698-716`; `vfe3/paper/runs.py:667-681`). Log axes drop nonpositive samples in `_series_panel`, `_labeled_series_panel`, `plot_trajectory` and the attention heatmaps (`vfe3/viz/figures.py:1093-1144, 1338, 2376-2377, 2497-2498`). Route gating of the legacy E-step figure family and the attention_estep replay is correct (`vfe3/viz/figures.py:358-400`; `vfe3/train.py:3473-3488`). `emit_registered_figures` publishes through atomic temps with leak cleanup (`vfe3/viz/specs.py:36-87`). The isolated plotting proxy `_IsolatedFigures` closes leaked figures and writes through sibling temps (`vfe3/run_artifacts.py:5305-5351`).

### performance

The active path forms no dense (B,N,N,K,K) Omega: `build_belief_transport` returns `CompactFactoredTransport` and the frame context keeps compact blocks (`vfe3/inference/e_step.py:159-166`; `vfe3/model/model.py:753-765`). On CUDA the frame exponential uses the sync-free fixed T18 schedule (7 static squarings at chart radius 50, d = 32) with a custom adjoint, while CPU keeps `torch.matrix_exp` (`vfe3/geometry/transport.py:1262-1291`). The frame exp and its float64 inverse run on unique token rows, and the one `torch.unique` sync is the documented trade-off, since a static buffer measured 2x slower (`vfe3/geometry/transport.py:1338-1361`). `CanonicalFrameContext` validates on unique rows through the gather mark on the active rope=None, pos_phi=none path, as a deferred host_check (`vfe3/contracts.py:81-187`). Every layer's content chart O' is evaluated once per forward in one batched float64 call (`vfe3/model/relational_operators.py:276-310`; `vfe3/model/model.py:1085`). The attention log prior and the comparison-RoPE rotation are cached across forwards (`vfe3/model/model.py:609-667`; `vfe3/model/relational_operators.py:1636-1660`). The prior handoff arithmetic is skipped exactly when prior_handoff_sigma = 0 and nothing captures (`vfe3/model/stack.py:35-59, 164-166`). The moment-matched covariance on diagonal beliefs takes the blockwise route with no K x K intermediate (`vfe3/model/relational_operators.py:1360-1361, 1364-1482`). The diagonal LayerNorm pushforward is an O(K) closed form with no Jacobian (`vfe3/model/block_mlp.py:367-386`). The coordinate block MLP with passthrough covariance forms no Jacobian (`vfe3/model/block_mlp.py:889-891`). The decode uses a fused chunked Triton CE with no (B,N,V) tensor, an IEEE-pinned cross term and no atomics (`vfe3/model/prior_bank.py:1480-1484`; `vfe3/kernels/kl_decode.py:81-168`). The target-range validation sync is moved to the pinned CPU loader handoff with a non_blocking copy (`vfe3/train.py:721-748`; `vfe3/model/prior_bank.py:1305-1327`). `train_step` folds the loss, grad finiteness, partition flags and clip norms into one host transfer, and the set norms are taken before the barrier (`vfe3/train.py:1017-1041`). AdamW is fused on CUDA (`vfe3/train.py:454-461`). `TrainingTimer` records CUDA events per step and synchronizes only at sampling (`vfe3/timing.py:62-109`). Diagnostics, per-layer diagnostics and evaluate run under `torch.no_grad` (`vfe3/model/model.py:2487, 2548`; `vfe3/train.py:1359`). amp_dtype=None enters no autocast object on the forward path (`vfe3/model/model.py:680-681, 705-706`). The pair-KL GEMM saves only token-linear inputs and recomputes its expansion at IEEE fp32 in backward (`vfe3/relational_scoring.py:480-518, 1264-1280`). The live run shows pipeline tokens/s equal to train-step tokens/s (10.7k), so data loading is not a bottleneck (`vfe3_runs/20261016-201802_wikitext-103_K160_block_glk_mlp_s6/metrics.csv`). phi_mstep_max_matrix_norm, phi_frame_condition_max, frame_pair_escalation_tol and pair_kl_gemm_tol are None on the launcher, so their per-step table passes and fallbacks do not run (`vfe3/config.py:747, 858`; `train_vfe3.py:337-339`).

### dead-code

All 161 `VFE3Config` fields have at least one read outside validation: irrep_min_rank, irrep_max_rank and irrep_spec_seed are consumed by the random irrep draw in `__post_init__` (`vfe3/config.py:1194-1200`), m_relational_score_lr and m_relational_content_lr are read through `f"m_relational_{role}_lr"` (`vfe3/train.py:352`), and trust_resume_checkpoint and val_diagnostic_batches are read through getattr (`vfe3/run_artifacts.py:2478`; `vfe3/train.py:4934`). An AST import scan found a production importer for every vfe3 module, and `agent_tooling/`, `vfe3/posthoc/holonomy.py` and the metric, monitor and retraction registries from earlier audits are gone. All five run_artifacts `collect_*` probes are invoked from finalize (`vfe3/run_artifacts.py:4078` and following). Every registered figure has a production caller by key or function name except `plot_ln3_symmetry_breaking`, which `vfe3/viz/report.py:20-22` documents as needing a two-arm experiment that has not been set up. Each per-device counter other than the GEMM-fallback one is read and reset by `train.py` (nonfinite tangent, SPD repair and ceiling, covariance ceiling, fp64 escalation, LN trace cap, mu-trust and decode logdet at `vfe3/train.py:83-93, 2110-2125, 4421-4425`). The counter snapshot functions return clones, so the window differences are not aliased (`vfe3/gradients/full_link.py:52-57`; `vfe3/relational_scoring.py:44-53`). The round-1 #16 Spearman duplicate is fixed, since `vfe3/viz/figures.py:5092` delegates to `metrics.spearman_rho`. The round-2 leftovers are fixed: the unreachable branch at `posthoc/maps.py:217` and `families/base.py` `expm1_neg_plus` no longer exist. A diff read of `vfe3/model/prior_bank.py:1386-1800` found that the diagonal and full chunked decode CE twins agree in centering offset, chunking and unigram add, with full excluding non-PD positions by design. The Triton chunked decode honors decode_av_precision (`vfe3/model/prior_bank.py:1364`). `vfe3/experiment.run_vfe` and `train_vfe3._run_once` agree on loader split policy, the data-seed generator, the post-build reseed, the final-validation handoff and the finalize arguments, with no behavioral drift found. The LayerNorm trace cap is applied in both the local and reference-coordinate pushforwards, and since head-block projection preserves the trace the cap is identical (`vfe3/model/block_mlp.py:430-447`; `vfe3/model/block.py:330-338`). The predictive_covariance_floor_mode validator tuples agree (`vfe3/config.py:938` against `vfe3/model/relational_operators.py:100`). The unused parameters of `projected_mean_distance_cost` and `projected_noisy_key_cost` are signature-uniform dispatch through ROUTING_COSTS (`vfe3/relational_scoring.py:1778-1791`), not dead knobs. gaussian_full and gaussian_diagonal giving bit-identical outputs on the default test profile is explained by skip_belief_sigma_update=True (`tests/profiles.py:134`) and not by the family being ignored; a probe found Lambda off-diagonal 0.86 and a dense full Sigma. The task's protected default-off instruments (gaussian_diagonal_exact, gaussian_frame_diagonal, regime_ii*, sigma_mc, efe_rollout, omega/phi_reflection, emit_expensive_diagnostics) no longer exist in vfe3 code, because the user retired them in commit 2f2772f (2026-10-02; `README.md:106-107`), so only the residue above remains. `zzzzz.py` is gitignored local scratch (`.gitignore:24`) and compiles, and `optimization_plan.md` is a tracked historical note already reported in the 2026-10-12 audit. The registry keys causal_noself and bch are reachable through config validation against the live registry or through tests, so they were not treated as orphans on string-search grounds alone.

### gradients-tests

All ten custom `autograd.Function` backwards pass `torch.autograd.gradcheck` in float64 (eps 1e-6, atol 1e-6 or 1e-5): `_EighDamped` through a spectral function (`vfe3/geometry/retraction.py:52`); `_SymmetricSpectralMap` for sqrt, inv_sqrt, log, exp_bounded and project with and without an upper bound, including a 1e-9 near-degenerate spectrum (`:270`); `_SymmetricSpectralMapPair` with both outputs and with one unused (`:304`); `_FixedScheduleMatrixExp` at ||A|| of about 0.7 and 3, gradgradcheck included, with forward equal to `torch.linalg.matrix_exp` to 2.2e-16 (`vfe3/geometry/transport.py:1195-1260`); `_SpectralCeiling` with eigenvalues straddling c (`vfe3/model/covariance_ceiling.py:57`); `_GramPrecision` for chol, P and logdet (`vfe3/relational_scoring.py:422`); and `_IEEEFP32Recompute` over `_projected_kl_expansion` (`vfe3/relational_scoring.py:480`). The `_IEEEFP32Recompute` bodies `_projected_kl_expansion` and `_diagonal_kl_expansion` are module-level functions with no closure over grad-requiring tensors, so no gradient is silently dropped (`vfe3/relational_scoring.py:1280, 1361`). A whole-model directional central-difference check in float64 on the tiny launcher route (live_config, K = 8, H = 2, L = 2) matches every parameter to at most 1.4e-5 relative once the gamma tie is broken, and the pure-path overrides match to 1.0e-6. About 40 toggle variants also match, with no unexplained zero or None gradient outside the pushforward/full-family case: minimal_agent exact, mean_field and spread_corrected; projected_gradient closed_form, euler and closed_form_profiled; rope comparison, frame, frame_value and none; learned pos_phi; learned temperature; noisy_query, noisy_key and mean_distance routing; predictive_covariance_checkpoint; decode_ce_checkpoint auto_aggregate; expected_likelihood, linear and second-order decode; gaussian_block; prototype_agents, canonical_frame, gaussian_template and gauge_gate MLP; delta_full; block_so, block_sl and block_co groups; shared tying; frozen and off transport; reference decode; attention_residual; lowrank and pure_gauge edges; per_type_hidden_bias; floor_mode relative; and fp64 escalation. The exact-zero gradients at init on gauge_gate fc1 (fc2 is zero-initialized, `vfe3/model/block_mlp.py:943-944`) and on edge_receiver_map and edge_site_basis come from zero-factor initializations, not severed paths. Every requires_grad parameter on the live route gets a non-None gradient, and decode_log_scale is frozen by design (`vfe3/model/model.py:388`). Every trainable parameter is in exactly one optimizer group, and the Fisher-table markers are unique ('mu' -> mu_embed, 'sigma' -> sigma_log_embed; `vfe3/train.py:292-316`). fp32 and fp64 analytic gradients at the K = 160, H = 5, L = 4 live shape agree to at most 1.4e-5 relative on every parameter except sigma_log_embed (1.2e-4), so the IEEE pins keep fp32 gradients accurate at init. The block_norm.weight central-FD mismatch (2.1%) at the all-ones gamma init is the amax tie in the trace-cap bound (`vfe3/model/block_mlp.py:496`); it vanishes (9e-13) after a 0.01 perturbation and is the documented, intended behavior of item 8 of `docs/audit-results_2026-10-15-instability.md`. The ceiling-anchor detach (`vfe3/model/covariance_ceiling.py:124`) makes the sigma_log_embed gradient differ from FD when the ceiling binds (rel 0.92 at c = 0.3), which is the deliberate 2026-10-15 remediation and not a defect. The floor anchor sigma_p is not detached through `vfe3/model/model.py:1086` -> `vfe3/inference/e_step.py:266` -> `RelationalOperators.floor_term` (`vfe3/model/relational_operators.py:939`), and it carries no gradient at init only because the decode variances are equal, so dL/dSigma_q = 0 exactly. `query_row_constant_grad=False` is set only under routing_kl_max_reference='row_min' (`vfe3/gradients/relational.py:104-105`), where the row constant cancels in the clamped gap, so it is exact. The fp64 escalation writes recomputed rows with an out-of-place `index_copy` (`vfe3/model/relational_operators.py:1323`), so the replaced float32 rows contribute no gradient. The Table Fisher step consumes the autograd gradient of the gathered rows only, and rows with n_v = 0 have zero gradient anyway (`vfe3/table_optim.py:134-210`). On test health, the conditional skip in `tests/test_audit_20261008_core.py:182` does not fire (the test passes); the clip spies in `tests/test_train.py:556-615` are non-vacuous because a sibling asserts the call set; the `except Exception: pass` in `tests/test_audit_20261010_core.py:239` cannot hide a failure, because `pytest.raises` still requires the deferred ValueError; and CUDA-only tests are registered in `tests/pytest_policy.py` and skip with a reason. `tests/test_live_route_profile.py`, `tests/test_covariance_ceiling_20261013.py`, `tests/test_predictive_covariance_fp64_escalation_20261015.py` and `tests/test_predictive_covariance_floor_mode_20261016.py` pass on CPU.

### gap-live-decode-triton-kernels

In the interpreter, the forward kernel at `vfe3/kernels/_kl_decode_triton.py:56-83` handles row masking (S = 3, 5, 7, 9 against BI = 2), column masking of padded tiles, the padded-column target-hit guard, the -inf online-max guard and multiple tiles per chunk (chunk = 530 > BV = 512), matching a float64 reference at fp32 rounding (rows 2e-8 to 1.4e-7) and agreeing in fp64 to at most 7.9e-17. In the backward kernel at `vfe3/kernels/_kl_decode_triton.py:86-123`, G = -0.5[centered>=0] w/tau, dper_pos = -sum_v G and dtau = tau^-2 sum w*kl match float64 autograd of the torch-equivalent reference (fp32 lhs/rhs/bias gradients 4e-8 to 5.6e-7, comparable to the torch fp32 route's errors; fp64 at most 1.3e-14). The clamp-boundary semantics at `vfe3/kernels/_kl_decode_triton.py:116` are correct: centered == 0 passes the gradient exactly as `torch.clamp(min=0)` backward does (max diff 8.9e-16 at an exact-zero construction). The NaN semantics at `vfe3/kernels/_kl_decode_triton.py:45` and `116` match torch, with a NaN row giving NaN CE and the same NaN-gradient pattern ([0,0,9,0,1] NaN counts on both). In `vfe3/kernels/kl_decode.py:97-124`, the S = 0 path, partial needs_input_grad, keep=True (kept A plus scratch G) and keep=False (recompute, with G overwriting A) give identical results. m and log_l are kept apart at `vfe3/kernels/kl_decode.py:118-124`, so at tau = 1e-3 the fused CE and gradients match the torch route's float64 error (rows 1.0e-7 on both). `_decode_ce_triton_rows` at `vfe3/model/prior_bank.py:1339-1384` builds rhs, bias, lhs, per_pos, u and tau elementwise identical to the torch chunk loop, including the fp64 a_v island (fp32 pieces cast after construction, as in the recursion of `_decode_av`). The keep accounting at `vfe3/model/prior_bank.py:1371-1379` uses the torch route's formula (inner = 2, per-chunk width, aggregate override) and differs only in using min(chunk, V) for the ragged last chunk, which is harmless. For the torch reference at `vfe3/model/prior_bank.py:1386-1533`, gradcheck on mu_q and sigma_q in float64 passes, decode-table gradients match central differences to at most 1.6e-9, and the chunked CE equals dense decode plus `F.cross_entropy` to at most 3.6e-15 (ragged chunks, ignored targets, unigram bias, tied and untied, keep and checkpoint). The max-centered chunk summaries and their cross-chunk combine at `vfe3/model/prior_bank.py:132-168` are correct, as covered by the gradcheck above. decode_log_scale is frozen at `vfe3/model/model.py:388`, so NEED_TAU is False on the live path. The analytic EL backward formulas at `vfe3/kernels/_expected_likelihood_triton.py:124-195` (mu_q, sigma_q, mu_v, sigma_v, u, tau) are correct, with an fp64 first-order match of 1.1e-16 and correct row and column masking. The EL Function at `vfe3/kernels/expected_likelihood.py:113-149` fails loudly under double backward, since its first gradient does not require grad. The interpreter, CPU-routing and never-imports-triton tests in `tests/test_kl_decode_triton_20261012.py` pass under `$CUDA_PYTHON` with CUDA hidden (4 passed), and `tests/test_decode_ce_checkpoint_gate_20260807.py` passes on CPU (22 passed).

### gap-paper-arms-and-multiseed

The closed-form Student t 0.975 quantile at `multiseed_analysis.py:203-239` matches tables for nu = 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 9, 30 to 7 digits (executed). The single-seed noise floor at `multiseed_analysis.py:189-200` is t_{n-1,0.975}*sqrt(2)*s, so the 2*SD floor of item 8 in `docs/audits/audit-2026-10-12.md` is fixed and no scipy is needed. `multiseed_analysis.py:242-253` and `:741-749` use the ddof=1 SD, and CV is restricted to positive ratio-scale keys (`:979-993`). In the request-manifest design at `multiseed_analysis.py:496-625`, failed, missing, duplicate, unexpected or unverifiable seeds make the design incomplete and withhold the aggregate, so there is no survivorship of failed seeds, and train/val/test data hashes and tokenizer identity are enforced. The curve bands at `multiseed_analysis.py:752-841` withhold a column when any seed lacks a finite value at an active step. `multiseed_analysis.py` was run read-only on `D:/DATA_KEEP/10-5-2026_Multiseed`, giving a complete design, test_ppl 87.194 +/- 0.326 and noise floor 1.985 (executed). The prediction band t_{n-1}*s*sqrt(1+1/n) at `vfe3/viz/figures.py:5740-5743` is the correct one-new-run interval. The debiased HSIC at `vfe3/semantics/multiseed.py:581-603` reduces correctly for centered rows (tr KL - n/(n-2) <diag K, diag L> + trK trL/((n-1)(n-2))), and the row-permuted null permutes ssr consistently. The per-word Jaccard and hypergeometric chance level at `vfe3/semantics/multiseed.py:555-570` are correct for k-subsets. Config differences and the data-order status (shared, distinct, partially_shared, unknown) at `vfe3/semantics/multiseed.py:255-320` are reported and stated in the markdown. `check_vfe_arm` at `paper_arms.py:637-661` confirms that every ready VFE arm on k128_alibi differs only in its declared and coerced fields (executed dry plan). Every baseline arm on k128_alibi passes the protocol-field check at `paper_arms.py:664-697`, and the post-norm arm declares the kept residual (executed). `seed_pairs` at `paper_arms.py:780-795` validates and deduplicates, the pairs are shared by every arm, and `vfe3/paper/tables.py:370-418` pairs on (seed, data_seed). `ladder_widths` at `paper_arms.py:1342-1345` gives whole-head widths 128..1024 at K = 128, d = 32. The decay predicates at `paper_arms.py:219-231` and `:608-614` read `resolved_hyperparameter`, so round-2 audit item C7 is fixed. The getattr fallback to True at `paper_arms.py:952` is unreachable because `matched` is always a `VFE3Config`, so the item in `docs/audit-results_2026-10-15-instability.md` is moot. The existing tf_matched_params and tf_matched_total runs (3 seeds each) at `paper_arms.py:1150-1184` pass reuse_mismatch, so the current TIER_A launch is not blocked (executed). `_alibi_already`, `_same_as_alibi_as_run`, `_no_kernel_routing` and `decision_comparator` at `paper_arms.py:297-333` resolve correctly on the ALiBi-without-RoPE base (executed). `tests/test_paper_arms_20260928.py` (15 passed) and `tests/test_multiseed.py` (53 passed) pass on CPU with one thread.

### gap-checkpoint-resume-exactness

The Fisher table M-step resume is exact: `TableFisherAdamW` and `TableFisherGaugeManifoldAdamW` are stateless for the tables (the base step runs with their grads hidden, so there are no AdamW slots, and eta and the trust radius are constructor-only), and the probe's step_8 checkpoints are bitwise equal with identical losses after resumes at steps 4 and 6 (`vfe3/table_optim.py:113-246`). In the LR schedule rebuild, initial_lr = base and LambdaLR(last_epoch = successful_updates-1) reproduce lr_t and the table `_schedule_factor` exactly, and the lr_* metrics columns match row for row (`vfe3/train.py:4329-4339`, `vfe3/table_optim.py:106-110`). The `GaugeManifoldAdamW` state_dict/load_state_dict persists phi_group_differential_mode, a fisher_group resume is bitwise exact, and pullback_group slots are rejected fail-closed (`vfe3/gauge_optim.py:1518-1555`, `vfe3/run_artifacts.py:1364-1451`). The live group metadata restamp `_optimizer_state_with_live_group_metadata` keeps the current config authoritative and carries successful_updates (`vfe3/run_artifacts.py:1708-1726`). The GradScaler fp16 scale and growth tracker round-trip, and the probe under amp_dtype='fp16' is bitwise equal including scaler_state (`vfe3/run_artifacts.py:1729-1757, 2262, 2659-2660`). For the data cursor, an epoch-boundary checkpoint (batches_consumed == steps_per_epoch) and a mid-epoch checkpoint under per-epoch window origins and a shuffled loader generator replay the same order, and data_state and window_offset are equal at step 8 (`vfe3/train.py:4447-4506, 4622-4635`; `vfe3/data/datasets.py:800-818`). The replay cost of the batch skip measured 0.095 ms/batch (B = 16, N = 128), about 5 s for a late-epoch wikitext-103 cursor, so it is not a performance issue (`vfe3/train.py:4494-4506`). For the global RNG, the checkpoint captures the CPU RNG after the eval block restores it, and the loader replay restores it again, equal at step 8 (`vfe3/train.py:4871, 4994, 4997-5029, 4483-4506`). The numerics counters (run totals and frame-projection counts carried in numerics_counters) are equal at step 8 for both resume points (`vfe3/train.py:2090-2157, 4426-4427, 5015-5018`). The best-model bookkeeping (best_val_ppl/best_step and the embedded best_model_bundle) round-trips under the live optimizer, and all three runs end at (5.940, 4) (`vfe3/run_artifacts.py:2228-2246, 2278-2388`). The metrics.csv rows of overlapping steps 6 and 8 are identical between continuous and resumed runs in every non-timing column, including tokens_seen and the attempted/skipped counters (`vfe3/train.py:5041-5300`). The process-global numerics policies set in `VFEModel.__init__` are re-derived from the current cfg when the resumed model is built, config drift on them is warned, and the fp32_matmul_precision=None leak across sweep cells is handled by `restored_fp32_matmul_precision` (`vfe3/model/model.py:264-276`, `ablation.py:2166`, `scaling.py:1169`). The frame condition bound after load (phi_frame_condition_max = 1e4 live) is a bitwise no-op on a bounded checkpoint (`vfe3/run_artifacts.py:2658`; the probe is exact). Multi-seed together with resume_from is refused in the launcher (`train_vfe3.py:692-695`). The Python random and numpy global RNGs are not used on the training path (only explicitly seeded generators in posthoc and semantics), so not checkpointing them is correct. The selected `test_checkpoint_resume.py` tests (-k resume_matches, shuffled_resume, pullback_group_resume, sequential_resume, restores_optimizer, restamps, best_val_state, cross_run_resume) gave 16 passed on CPU.

### gap-opt-in-readout-and-link-routes

The mean-field label step softmax(log pi - KL(N(a,A)||N(b_j,B_j))) at `vfe3/model/minimal_agent.py:538-600` is the exact CAVI update of q(J^h) given q(X), with dropped j-independent terms -d/2(1+log 2pi) - 1/2 log det A. The power-posterior fusion at `vfe3/model/minimal_agent.py:518-535` and `603-628` matches the CAVI q(X) optimum (precision C^-1 + sum_h S_h^T Lambda_h S_h, information sum_h S_h^T eta_h), and the information_step algebra M = (A + Lambda^-1)^-1 = L E^-1 L^T and u = L E^-1 (L^-1 eta - L^T a) was checked. Later CAVI sweeps at `vfe3/model/minimal_agent.py:1409-1436` relabel at the previous posterior query moments and refuse from the input moments, so each sweep is block coordinate ascent, and the RoPE rotate-in and rotate-back of Lambda and eta (`1399-1403`) match the gradient of the rotated record. The algebra of the exact-path pair tiling at `vfe3/model/minimal_agent.py:351-511` (online log-sum-exp with Chan's centered update, detached stabilizers, skipped fully masked tiles) was checked, and the existing tiling tests cover the values. The ADF head step M = Lambda_bar - R at `vfe3/model/minimal_agent.py:1350-1365` reproduces the mixture covariance C - K(Lambda_bar - R)K^T. The ELBO terms at `vfe3/model/minimal_agent.py:1012-1099` were checked (E_q log N(SX; b, B) = -KL - d/2(1+log 2pi) - 1/2 log det A^+, normalized pi_hat, 0 log 0 = 0, no_record heads dropped, spread_corrected A^+). The ADF evidence at `vfe3/model/minimal_agent.py:1102-1121` is sum_h logsumexp_j(log pi_hat - c_ij) - d/2 log 2pi, exact for one head and for uncoupled heads. At `vfe3/model/minimal_agent.py:1176-1197`, the IS log-weight log N(X;xi,C) - log q(X) and the label-summed record log-likelihood are algebraically correct, and the "biased downward" claim and E log p_hat >= E_q log w >= L hold (Jensen per X). The M-step objective F(n) at `vfe3/model/minimal_agent.py:770-869`, its derivatives, the bracket [0, U] and the boundary flag logic are correct, and `tests/test_t6_channel_noise_mstep.py` passes (9 tests). The float64 recompute at `vfe3/model/relational_operators.py:1060-1080` discards the float32 attempt, so there is no backward through failed factorizations, and raises on a float64 failure; the A^h certificate uses the input S_h C S_h^T, as documented. The exact posterior weights, the mean xi + C u, the cancellation-free C^+ = C Psi + C R C, the block-diagonal moment projection and the log_evidence normalization at `vfe3/model/prototype_agents.py:347-480` were checked. The gradient of the exact EM objective at `vfe3/model/prototype_agents.py:519-580` and `vfe3/model/block_mlp.py:1477-1523` equals grad log p(xi|theta) by Fisher's identity (component moments m_ia and diag(C W^-1 Lambda_a) checked), and the mean-field EM term equals the ELBO gradient at the final CAVI state. The mean-field label (diag S only) and the power posterior P = D^-1/2 B^-1 D^1/2 C, delta = P(eta - D xi), at `vfe3/model/prototype_agents.py:584-682` match the CAVI optima. The prototype ELBO decomposition at `vfe3/model/prototype_agents.py:717-755` is correct, and `vfe3/model/block_mlp.py:1569-1579` passes the matching q for each inference mode. The closed-form gradients at `vfe3/gradients/full_link.py:525-601` match autograd of `full_link_cost` to 3.6e-15 (executed probe, dense family, 200 instances), and the profiled-context envelope identity was checked. The dense closed-form and damped updates at `vfe3/gradients/full_link.py:604-671` stay SPD and never raised the frozen energy at rho in {0.1, 0.5, 0.9, 1} over 200 random instances (executed). At `vfe3/gradients/full_link.py:684-831`, D(a) along the minimizer curve, its slopes (r^2/(l+a)^3, l^2/(2a(l+a)^2), Lc^2/(2a den^2)), their monotonicity and the certified-step lower bound on h' were checked. The channel-likelihood context Lambda, eta and c = alpha at `vfe3/gradients/full_link.py:401-522` match E_q[-log N(P_h xi; v, R)] + alpha KL(q||p), and the alpha -> 0 inverse-measurement minimizer equals the kernel mean U O' z. The latent_sources constant at `vfe3/model/edge_links.py:515-598` (head-pair gram, spread term, residual cross term 2 sum_h beta_ii tr(O'_h T_h C) and kappa) and the latent energy were checked by hand, and `tests/test_latent_links_reference_20261005.py` passes (85 tests). The fixed point of A <- A0 - sigma_L^2 grad E at `vfe3/model/edge_links.py:730-794` is the stationary point of F_i, receivers decouple, and masked pairs pass phi_1(0) with zero argument gradient (causal). The lowrank and pure-gauge Phi, g_i and the reference message factorization g O'(z + T A y) at `vfe3/model/edge_links.py:124-146, 196-214` were checked against exp(AB^T) = I + A phi_1(B^T A) B^T. `tests/test_audit_20261012_round2_evidence.py` passes (5 tests) in the current tree.
